# Kimi Code parity matrix

Reference: MoonshotAI/kimi-code at `/workspace/refs/kimi-code`, commit `eb2035f`.
Subject: fx at `/workspace/fx/fx`, branch main.

Each row compares one user-facing surface. Status is `present` (same behaviour and names), `partial` (surface exists, behaviour or names differ), or `missing`. Rows are driven to `present` one surface per commit, each with a red witness before the port and a green witness after it.

Counts below are recounted from the rows of this file at the parity audit merge; see docs/PENDING_VALIDATION.md, Phase A audit merge.

| Surface | Rows | Present | Partial | Missing |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Built-in tools | 44 | 26 | 18 | 0 |
| Slash commands | 45 | 8 | 18 | 19 |
| Instructions and system prompt | 28 | 11 | 14 | 3 |
| Config files and keys | 48 | 13 | 32 | 3 |
| MCP client | 34 | 13 | 17 | 4 |
| Hooks | 33 | 14 | 9 | 10 |
| Permission modes | 32 | 2 | 25 | 5 |
| Skills and discovery | 32 | 8 | 15 | 9 |
| Subagents and swarms | 26 | 5 | 15 | 6 |
| Print and ACP modes | 35 | 12 | 13 | 10 |
| Plugins | 33 | 10 | 14 | 9 |
| Sessions and resume | 30 | 10 | 18 | 2 |
| Phase A double-Esc and help panel | 3 | 1 | 2 | 0 |
| Total | 423 | 133 | 210 | 80 |
| Claude Code docs CC1 Hooks, permissions, memory | 59 | 2 | 37 | 20 |
| Claude Code docs CC2 MCP, skills, subagents, plugins | 72 | 0 | 30 | 42 |
| Claude Code docs CC3 Built-in tools, sessions, interactive mode, defaults | 112 | 9 | 51 | 52 |
| Claude Code docs CC4 Commands, CLI, settings, keybindings, output modes | 155 | 10 | 50 | 95 |
| Claude Code docs total (counted separately from Kimi) | 398 | 21 | 168 | 209 |

Gap: the approval panel offers approve or deny only. The fixed choices (Approve, Reject, Reject and Exit, Revise) and the model's options (up to three, max_options in exit_plan_mode.zig) never reach the permission request, so raising the cap alone shows nothing. Decision: three fixed choices plus up to three model options (six in all), shown once the request carries an option list |
